LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c39627651", "text": "Article 4\nAmendments to Annex I", "para_index": 290, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c384f8620", "text": "The Commission is empowered to adopt delegated acts in accordance with Article 73 to amend the list of techniques and approaches listed in Annex I, in order to update that list to market and technological developments on the basis of characteristics that are similar to the techniques and approaches listed therein.", "para_index": 291, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cfb4004d2", "text": "PROHIBITED ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE PRACTICES", "para_index": 292, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c68a23027", "text": "1.The following artificial intelligence practices shall be prohibited:", "para_index": 293, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c906d444c", "text": "(a)the placing on the market, putting into service or use of an AI system that deploys subliminal techniques beyond a person’s consciousness in order to materially distort a person’s behaviour in a manner that causes or is likely to cause that person or another person physical or psychological harm;", "para_index": 294, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c3a85d5d0", "text": "(b)the placing on the market, putting into service or use of an AI system that exploits any of the vulnerabilities of a specific group of persons due to their age, physical or mental disability, in order to materially distort the behaviour of a person pertaining to that group in a manner that causes or is likely to cause that person or another person physical or psychological harm;", "para_index": 295, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca3e026aa", "text": "(c)the placing on the market, putting into service or use of AI systems by public authorities or on their behalf for the evaluation or classification of the trustworthiness of natural persons over a certain period of time based on their social behaviour or known or predicted personal or personality characteristics, with the social score leading to either or both of the following:", "para_index": 296, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "analysis", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd3a04a3f", "text": "(i)detrimental or unfavourable treatment of certain natural persons or whole groups thereof in social contexts which are unrelated to the contexts in which the data was originally generated or collected;", "para_index": 297, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c31f16688", "text": "(ii)detrimental or unfavourable treatment of certain natural persons or whole groups thereof that is unjustified or disproportionate to their social behaviour or its gravity;", "para_index": 298, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca464dc59", "text": "(d)the use of ‘real-time’ remote biometric identification systems in publicly accessible spaces for the purpose of law enforcement, unless and in as far as such use is strictly necessary for one of the following objectives:", "para_index": 299, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c63d00121", "text": "(i)the targeted search for specific potential victims of crime, including missing children;", "para_index": 300, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c853f9ed5", "text": "(ii)the prevention of a specific, substantial and imminent threat to the life or physical safety of natural persons or of a terrorist attack;", "para_index": 301, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1cba4298", "text": "(iii)the detection, localisation, identification or prosecution of a perpetrator or suspect of a criminal offence referred to in Article 2(2) of Council Framework Decision 2002/584/JHA [62](https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/?uri=CELEX:52021PC0206#footnote63) and punishable in the Member State concerned by a custodial sentence or a detention order for a maximum period of at least three years, as determined by the law of that Member State.", "para_index": 302, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "data", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cbbd35de7", "text": "2.The use of ‘real-time’ remote biometric identification systems in publicly accessible spaces for the purpose of law enforcement for any of the objectives referred to in paragraph 1 point d) shall take into account the following elements:", "para_index": 303, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c358b2233", "text": "(a)the nature of the situation giving rise to the possible use, in particular the seriousness, probability and scale of the harm caused in the absence of the use of the system;", "para_index": 304, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c92651156", "text": "(b)the consequences of the use of the system for the rights and freedoms of all persons concerned, in particular the seriousness, probability and scale of those consequences.", "para_index": 305, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf5f07f83", "text": "In addition, the use of ‘real-time’ remote biometric identification systems in publicly accessible spaces for the purpose of law enforcement for any of the objectives referred to in paragraph 1 point d) shall comply with necessary and proportionate safeguards and conditions in relation to the use, in particular as regards the temporal, geographic and personal limitations.", "para_index": 306, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc31b886f", "text": "3.As regards paragraphs 1, point (d) and 2, each individual use for the purpose of law enforcement of a ‘real-time’ remote biometric identification system in publicly accessible spaces shall be subject to a prior authorisation granted by a judicial authority or by an independent administrative authority of the Member State in which the use is to take place, issued upon a reasoned request and in accordance with the detailed rules of national law referred to in paragraph 4. However, in a duly just", "para_index": 307, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf3bfd8f1", "text": "The competent judicial or administrative authority shall only grant the authorisation where it is satisfied, based on objective evidence or clear indications presented to it, that the use of the ‘real-time’ remote biometric identification system at issue is necessary for and proportionate to achieving one of the objectives specified in paragraph 1, point (d), as identified in the request. In deciding on the request, the competent judicial or administrative authority shall take into account the e", "para_index": 308, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ced8d044e", "text": "4.A Member State may decide to provide for the possibility to fully or partially authorise the use of ‘real-time’ remote biometric identification systems in publicly accessible spaces for the purpose of law enforcement within the limits and under the conditions listed in paragraphs 1, point (d), 2 and 3. That Member State shall lay down in its national law the necessary detailed rules for the request, issuance and exercise of, as well as supervision relating to, the authorisations referred to in", "para_index": 309, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c886dfc86", "text": "CLASSIFICATION OF AI SYSTEMS AS HIGH-RISK", "para_index": 310, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ccf55adbe", "text": "Article 6\nClassification rules for high-risk AI systems", "para_index": 311, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c8ba6bd17", "text": "1.Irrespective of whether an AI system is placed on the market or put into service independently from the products referred to in points (a) and (b), that AI system shall be considered high-risk where both of the following conditions are fulfilled:", "para_index": 312, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c5dbc5543", "text": "(a)the AI system is intended to be used as a safety component of a product, or is itself a product, covered by the Union harmonisation legislation listed in Annex II;", "para_index": 313, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c59a75686", "text": "(b)the product whose safety component is the AI system, or the AI system itself as a product, is required to undergo a third-party conformity assessment with a view to the placing on the market or putting into service of that product pursuant to the Union harmonisation legislation listed in Annex II.", "para_index": 314, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c27ba6ff7", "text": "2.In addition to the high-risk AI systems referred to in paragraph 1, AI systems referred to in Annex III shall also be considered high-risk.", "para_index": 315, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd4d4ed5b", "text": "Article 7\nAmendments to Annex III", "para_index": 316, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c234f8a4e", "text": "1.The Commission is empowered to adopt delegated acts in accordance with Article 73 to update the list in Annex III by adding high-risk AI systems where both of the following conditions are fulfilled:", "para_index": 317, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c672c652e", "text": "(a)the AI systems are intended to be used in any of the areas listed in points 1 to 8 of Annex III;", "para_index": 318, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c86f0a941", "text": "(b)the AI systems pose a risk of harm to the health and safety, or a risk of adverse impact on fundamental rights, that is, in respect of its severity and probability of occurrence, equivalent to or greater than the risk of harm or of adverse impact posed by the high-risk AI systems already referred to in Annex III.", "para_index": 319, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cda26bcc2", "text": "2.When assessing for the purposes of paragraph 1 whether an AI system poses a risk of harm to the health and safety or a risk of adverse impact on fundamental rights that is equivalent to or greater than the risk of harm posed by the high-risk AI systems already referred to in Annex III, the Commission shall take into account the following criteria:", "para_index": 320, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd753222a", "text": "(a)the intended purpose of the AI system;", "para_index": 321, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb3057d8d", "text": "(b)the extent to which an AI system has been used or is likely to be used;", "para_index": 322, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c58aa9d97", "text": "(c)the extent to which the use of an AI system has already caused harm to the health and safety or adverse impact on the fundamental rights or has given rise to significant concerns in relation to the materialisation of such harm or adverse impact, as demonstrated by reports or documented allegations submitted to national competent authorities;", "para_index": 323, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c51bd0805", "text": "(d)the potential extent of such harm or such adverse impact, in particular in terms of its intensity and its ability to affect a plurality of persons;", "para_index": 324, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c0730ea41", "text": "(e)the extent to which potentially harmed or adversely impacted persons are dependent on the outcome produced with an AI system, in particular because for practical or legal reasons it is not reasonably possible to opt-out from that outcome;", "para_index": 325, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c4885557c", "text": "(f)the extent to which potentially harmed or adversely impacted persons are in a vulnerable position in relation to the user of an AI system, in particular due to an imbalance of power, knowledge, economic or social circumstances, or age;", "para_index": 326, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cde3b6b39", "text": "(g)the extent to which the outcome produced with an AI system is easily reversible, whereby outcomes having an impact on the health or safety of persons shall not be considered as easily reversible;", "para_index": 327, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c000c7d21", "text": "(h)the extent to which existing Union legislation provides for:", "para_index": 328, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c240141ee", "text": "(i)effective measures of redress in relation to the risks posed by an AI system, with the exclusion of claims for damages;", "para_index": 329, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c178f6903", "text": "(ii)effective measures to prevent or substantially minimise those risks.", "para_index": 330, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ccb2e8d7c", "text": "requirements for high-risk Ai systems", "para_index": 331, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb8cd46be", "text": "Article 8\nCompliance with the requirements", "para_index": 332, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf4e9c91c", "text": "1.High-risk AI systems shall comply with the requirements established in this Chapter.", "para_index": 333, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb3155075", "text": "2.The intended purpose of the high-risk AI system and the risk management system referred to in Article 9 shall be taken into account when ensuring compliance with those requirements.", "para_index": 334, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c6bd7e342", "text": "Article 9\nRisk management system", "para_index": 335, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c99f83a5c", "text": "1.A risk management system shall be established, implemented, documented and maintained in relation to high-risk AI systems.", "para_index": 336, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cdeb65e9f", "text": "2.The risk management system shall consist of a continuous iterative process run throughout the entire lifecycle of a high-risk AI system, requiring regular systematic updating. It shall comprise the following steps:", "para_index": 337, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca17d4596", "text": "(a)identification and analysis of the known and foreseeable risks associated with each high-risk AI system;", "para_index": 338, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c26aef408", "text": "(b)estimation and evaluation of the risks that may emerge when the high-risk AI system is used in accordance with its intended purpose and under conditions of reasonably foreseeable misuse;", "para_index": 339, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c143d74ab", "text": "(c)evaluation of other possibly arising risks based on the analysis of data gathered from the post-market monitoring system referred to in Article 61;", "para_index": 340, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cbd576dce", "text": "(d)adoption of suitable risk management measures in accordance with the provisions of the following paragraphs.", "para_index": 341, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c37fc5222", "text": "3.The risk management measures referred to in paragraph 2, point (d) shall give due consideration to the effects and possible interactions resulting from the combined application of the requirements set out in this Chapter 2. They shall take into account the generally acknowledged state of the art, including as reflected in relevant harmonised standards or common specifications.", "para_index": 342, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd0c6b1fd", "text": "4.The risk management measures referred to in paragraph 2, point (d) shall be such that any residual risk associated with each hazard as well as the overall residual risk of the high-risk AI systems is judged acceptable, provided that the high-risk AI system is used in accordance with its intended purpose or under conditions of reasonably foreseeable misuse. Those residual risks shall be communicated to the user.", "para_index": 343, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c0f87d68f", "text": "In identifying the most appropriate risk management measures, the following shall be ensured:", "para_index": 344, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc77aed18", "text": "(a)elimination or reduction of risks as far as possible through adequate design and development;", "para_index": 345, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c5c100145", "text": "(b)where appropriate, implementation of adequate mitigation and control measures in relation to risks that cannot be eliminated;", "para_index": 346, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb1a4ad04", "text": "(c)provision of adequate information pursuant to Article 13, in particular as regards the risks referred to in paragraph 2, point (b) of this Article, and, where appropriate, training to users.", "para_index": 347, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd8a502d4", "text": "In eliminating or reducing risks related to the use of the high-risk AI system, due consideration shall be given to the technical knowledge, experience, education, training to be expected by the user and the environment in which the system is intended to be used.", "para_index": 348, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c2f13dcdf", "text": "5.High-risk AI systems shall be tested for the purposes of identifying the most appropriate risk management measures. Testing shall ensure that high-risk AI systems perform consistently for their intended purpose and they are in compliance with the requirements set out in this Chapter.", "para_index": 349, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce395f987", "text": "6.Testing procedures shall be suitable to achieve the intended purpose of the AI system and do not need to go beyond what is necessary to achieve that purpose.", "para_index": 350, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c94e1dfed", "text": "7.The testing of the high-risk AI systems shall be performed, as appropriate, at any point in time throughout the development process, and, in any event, prior to the placing on the market or the putting into service. Testing shall be made against preliminarily defined metrics and probabilistic thresholds that are appropriate to the intended purpose of the high-risk AI system.", "para_index": 351, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c927e3a4e", "text": "8.When implementing the risk management system described in paragraphs 1 to 7, specific consideration shall be given to whether the high-risk AI system is likely to be accessed by or have an impact on children.", "para_index": 352, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc071c1d0", "text": "9.For credit institutions regulated by Directive 2013/36/EU, the aspects described in paragraphs 1 to 8 shall be part of the risk management procedures established by those institutions pursuant to Article 74 of that Directive.", "para_index": 353, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c534cc5bf", "text": "Article 10\nData and data governance", "para_index": 354, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb080090b", "text": "1.High-risk AI systems which make use of techniques involving the training of models with data shall be developed on the basis of training, validation and testing data sets that meet the quality criteria referred to in paragraphs 2 to 5.", "para_index": 355, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce8cde050", "text": "2.Training, validation and testing data sets shall be subject to appropriate data governance and management practices. Those practices shall concern in particular,", "para_index": 356, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c4f8dc7cd", "text": "(a)the relevant design choices;", "para_index": 357, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c131a39e4", "text": "(c)relevant data preparation processing operations, such as annotation, labelling, cleaning, enrichment and aggregation;", "para_index": 358, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c4688f3e8", "text": "(d)the formulation of relevant assumptions, notably with respect to the information that the data are supposed to measure and represent;", "para_index": 359, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cca4ed46f", "text": "(e)a prior assessment of the availability, quantity and suitability of the data sets that are needed;", "para_index": 360, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cff2cd8ca", "text": "(f)examination in view of possible biases;", "para_index": 361, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c977c2060", "text": "(g)the identification of any possible data gaps or shortcomings, and how those gaps and shortcomings can be addressed.", "para_index": 362, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce695e5a5", "text": "3.Training, validation and testing data sets shall be relevant, representative, free of errors and complete. They shall have the appropriate statistical properties, including, where applicable, as regards the persons or groups of persons on which the high-risk AI system is intended to be used. These characteristics of the data sets may be met at the level of individual data sets or a combination thereof.", "para_index": 363, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd6194607", "text": "4.Training, validation and testing data sets shall take into account, to the extent required by the intended purpose, the characteristics or elements that are particular to the specific geographical, behavioural or functional setting within which the high-risk AI system is intended to be used.", "para_index": 364, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cacc328a5", "text": "5.To the extent that it is strictly necessary for the purposes of ensuring bias monitoring, detection and correction in relation to the high-risk AI systems, the providers of such systems may process special categories of personal data referred to in Article 9(1) of Regulation (EU) 2016/679, Article 10 of Directive (EU) 2016/680 and Article 10(1) of Regulation (EU) 2018/1725, subject to appropriate safeguards for the fundamental rights and freedoms of natural persons, including technical limitat", "para_index": 365, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c6231ac34", "text": "6.Appropriate data governance and management practices shall apply for the development of high-risk AI systems other than those which make use of techniques involving the training of models in order to ensure that those high-risk AI systems comply with paragraph 2.", "para_index": 366, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ccf6eaecd", "text": "Article 11\nTechnical documentation", "para_index": 367, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cccb9a806", "text": "1.The technical documentation of a high-risk AI system shall be drawn up before that system is placed on the market or put into service and shall be kept up-to date.", "para_index": 368, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc30f7849", "text": "The technical documentation shall be drawn up in such a way to demonstrate that the high-risk AI system complies with the requirements set out in this Chapter and provide national competent authorities and notified bodies with all the necessary information to assess the compliance of the AI system with those requirements. It shall contain, at a minimum, the elements set out in Annex IV.", "para_index": 369, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cbc217efc", "text": "2.Where a high-risk AI system related to a product, to which the legal acts listed in Annex II, section A apply, is placed on the market or put into service one single technical documentation shall be drawn up containing all the information set out in Annex IV as well as the information required under those legal acts.", "para_index": 370, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c82653f26", "text": "3.The Commission is empowered to adopt delegated acts in accordance with Article 73 to amend Annex IV where necessary to ensure that, in the light of technical progress, the technical documentation provides all the necessary information to assess the compliance of the system with the requirements set out in this Chapter.", "para_index": 371, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c70a055ee", "text": "Article 12\nRecord-keeping", "para_index": 372, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c9971451a", "text": "1.High-risk AI systems shall be designed and developed with capabilities enabling the automatic recording of events (‘logs’) while the high-risk AI systems is operating. Those logging capabilities shall conform to recognised standards or common specifications.", "para_index": 373, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca544c142", "text": "2.The logging capabilities shall ensure a level of traceability of the AI system’s functioning throughout its lifecycle that is appropriate to the intended purpose of the system.", "para_index": 374, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce4219724", "text": "3.In particular, logging capabilities shall enable the monitoring of the operation of the high-risk AI system with respect to the occurrence of situations that may result in the AI system presenting a risk within the meaning of Article 65(1) or lead to a substantial modification, and facilitate the post-market monitoring referred to in Article 61.", "para_index": 375, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c8adfe87f", "text": "4.For high-risk AI systems referred to in paragraph 1, point (a) of Annex III, the logging capabilities shall provide, at a minimum:", "para_index": 376, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1100501d", "text": "(a)recording of the period of each use of the system (start date and time and end date and time of each use);", "para_index": 377, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c09426452", "text": "(b)the reference database against which input data has been checked by the system;", "para_index": 378, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c0ba35f63", "text": "(c)the input data for which the search has led to a match;", "para_index": 379, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cdb61d02e", "text": "(d)the identification of the natural persons involved in the verification of the results, as referred to in Article 14 (5).", "para_index": 380, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c143c6138", "text": "Article 13\nTransparency and provision of information to users", "para_index": 381, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c4a31fb84", "text": "1.High-risk AI systems shall be designed and developed in such a way to ensure that their operation is sufficiently transparent to enable users to interpret the system’s output and use it appropriately. An appropriate type and degree of transparency shall be ensured, with a view to achieving compliance with the relevant obligations of the user and of the provider set out in Chapter 3 of this Title.", "para_index": 382, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c38737198", "text": "2.High-risk AI systems shall be accompanied by instructions for use in an appropriate digital format or otherwise that include concise, complete, correct and clear information that is relevant, accessible and comprehensible to users.", "para_index": 383, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c5def0132", "text": "3.The information referred to in paragraph 2 shall specify:", "para_index": 384, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c776e2442", "text": "(a)the identity and the contact details of the provider and, where applicable, of its authorised representative;", "para_index": 385, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb5966096", "text": "(b)the characteristics, capabilities and limitations of performance of the high-risk AI system, including:", "para_index": 386, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c63d2dd2a", "text": "(i)its intended purpose;", "para_index": 387, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c53ba10b9", "text": "(ii)the level of accuracy, robustness and cybersecurity referred to in Article 15 against which the high-risk AI system has been tested and validated and which can be expected, and any known and foreseeable circumstances that may have an impact on that expected level of accuracy, robustness and cybersecurity;", "para_index": 388, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c00b9cdc8", "text": "(iii)any known or foreseeable circumstance, related to the use of the high-risk AI system in accordance with its intended purpose or under conditions of reasonably foreseeable misuse, which may lead to risks to the health and safety or fundamental rights;", "para_index": 389, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c8a67d0e5", "text": "(iv)its performance as regards the persons or groups of persons on which the system is intended to be used;", "para_index": 390, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c091f1cf3", "text": "(v)when appropriate, specifications for the input data, or any other relevant information in terms of the training, validation and testing data sets used, taking into account the intended purpose of the AI system.", "para_index": 391, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c274a6d25", "text": "(c)the changes to the high-risk AI system and its performance which have been pre-determined by the provider at the moment of the initial conformity assessment, if any;", "para_index": 392, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c844759a7", "text": "(d)the human oversight measures referred to in Article 14, including the technical measures put in place to facilitate the interpretation of the outputs of AI systems by the users;", "para_index": 393, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c0737883b", "text": "(e)the expected lifetime of the high-risk AI system and any necessary maintenance and care measures to ensure the proper functioning of that AI system, including as regards software updates.", "para_index": 394, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c25562c67", "text": "Article 14\nHuman oversight", "para_index": 395, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c595fcd3d", "text": "1.High-risk AI systems shall be designed and developed in such a way, including with appropriate human-machine interface tools, that they can be effectively overseen by natural persons during the period in which the AI system is in use.", "para_index": 396, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c41cc187c", "text": "2.Human oversight shall aim at preventing or minimising the risks to health, safety or fundamental rights that may emerge when a high-risk AI system is used in accordance with its intended purpose or under conditions of reasonably foreseeable misuse, in particular when such risks persist notwithstanding the application of other requirements set out in this Chapter.", "para_index": 397, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c5814e9d0", "text": "3.Human oversight shall be ensured through either one or all of the following measures:", "para_index": 398, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c63eecb82", "text": "(a)identified and built, when technically feasible, into the high-risk AI system by the provider before it is placed on the market or put into service;", "para_index": 399, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cece52815", "text": "(b)identified by the provider before placing the high-risk AI system on the market or putting it into service and that are appropriate to be implemented by the user.", "para_index": 400, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c7992ae1f", "text": "4.The measures referred to in paragraph 3 shall enable the individuals to whom human oversight is assigned to do the following, as appropriate to the circumstances:", "para_index": 401, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf3fc358f", "text": "(a)fully understand the capacities and limitations of the high-risk AI system and be able to duly monitor its operation, so that signs of anomalies, dysfunctions and unexpected performance can be detected and addressed as soon as possible;", "para_index": 402, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c5c8eb390", "text": "(b)remain aware of the possible tendency of automatically relying or over-relying on the output produced by a high-risk AI system (‘automation bias’), in particular for high-risk AI systems used to provide information or recommendations for decisions to be taken by natural persons;", "para_index": 403, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c533e5b40", "text": "(c)be able to correctly interpret the high-risk AI system’s output, taking into account in particular the characteristics of the system and the interpretation tools and methods available;", "para_index": 404, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c3e916a13", "text": "(d)be able to decide, in any particular situation, not to use the high-risk AI system or otherwise disregard, override or reverse the output of the high-risk AI system;", "para_index": 405, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca0e94223", "text": "(e)be able to intervene on the operation of the high-risk AI system or interrupt the system through a “stop” button or a similar procedure.", "para_index": 406, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c992f859b", "text": "5.For high-risk AI systems referred to in point 1(a) of Annex III, the measures referred to in paragraph 3 shall be such as to ensure that, in addition, no action or decision is taken by the user on the basis of the identification resulting from the system unless this has been verified and confirmed by at least two natural persons.", "para_index": 407, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc2b5a350", "text": "Article 15\nAccuracy, robustness and cybersecurity", "para_index": 408, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1f9893c4", "text": "1.High-risk AI systems shall be designed and developed in such a way that they achieve, in the light of their intended purpose, an appropriate level of accuracy, robustness and cybersecurity, and perform consistently in those respects throughout their lifecycle.", "para_index": 409, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1d1433c5", "text": "2.The levels of accuracy and the relevant accuracy metrics of high-risk AI systems shall be declared in the accompanying instructions of use.", "para_index": 410, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cacac017e", "text": "3.High-risk AI systems shall be resilient as regards errors, faults or inconsistencies that may occur within the system or the environment in which the system operates, in particular due to their interaction with natural persons or other systems.", "para_index": 411, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cfdec8c7a", "text": "The robustness of high-risk AI systems may be achieved through technical redundancy solutions, which may include backup or fail-safe plans.", "para_index": 412, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c2715ad2c", "text": "High-risk AI systems that continue to learn after being placed on the market or put into service shall be developed in such a way to ensure that possibly biased outputs due to outputs used as an input for future operations (‘feedback loops’) are duly addressed with appropriate mitigation measures.", "para_index": 413, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c33d1984a", "text": "4.High-risk AI systems shall be resilient as regards attempts by unauthorised third parties to alter their use or performance by exploiting the system vulnerabilities.", "para_index": 414, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c170fd69a", "text": "The technical solutions aimed at ensuring the cybersecurity of high-risk AI systems shall be appropriate to the relevant circumstances and the risks.", "para_index": 415, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce58ce8fb", "text": "The technical solutions to address AI specific vulnerabilities shall include, where appropriate, measures to prevent and control for attacks trying to manipulate the training dataset (‘data poisoning’), inputs designed to cause the model to make a mistake (‘adversarial examples’), or model flaws.", "para_index": 416, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "caaed6089", "text": "OBLIGATIONS OF PROVIDERS AND USERS OF HIGH-RISK AI SYSTEMS and other parties", "para_index": 417, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c6fba2596", "text": "Article 16\nObligations of providers of high-risk AI systems", "para_index": 418, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c18253629", "text": "Providers of high-risk AI systems shall:", "para_index": 419, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c59e233f7", "text": "(a)ensure that their high-risk AI systems are compliant with the requirements set out in Chapter 2 of this Title;", "para_index": 420, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c723639e1", "text": "(b)have a quality management system in place which complies with Article 17;", "para_index": 421, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca74f52a1", "text": "(c)draw-up the technical documentation of the high-risk AI system;", "para_index": 422, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc350525d", "text": "(d)when under their control, keep the logs automatically generated by their high-risk AI systems;", "para_index": 423, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cef18efd7", "text": "(e)ensure that the high-risk AI system undergoes the relevant conformity assessment procedure, prior to its placing on the market or putting into service;", "para_index": 424, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1e5166c0", "text": "(f)comply with the registration obligations referred to in Article 51;", "para_index": 425, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c23dbd582", "text": "(g)take the necessary corrective actions, if the high-risk AI system is not in conformity with the requirements set out in Chapter 2 of this Title;", "para_index": 426, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c207b8666", "text": "(h)inform the national competent authorities of the Member States in which they made the AI system available or put it into service and, where applicable, the notified body of the non-compliance and of any corrective actions taken;", "para_index": 427, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb83fce93", "text": "(i)to affix the CE marking to their high-risk AI systems to indicate the conformity with this Regulation in accordance with Article 49;", "para_index": 428, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c17d12878", "text": "(j)upon request of a national competent authority, demonstrate the conformity of the high-risk AI system with the requirements set out in Chapter 2 of this Title.", "para_index": 429, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc22f74cb", "text": "Article 17\nQuality management system", "para_index": 430, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce8b930b1", "text": "1.Providers of high-risk AI systems shall put a quality management system in place that ensures compliance with this Regulation. That system shall be documented in a systematic and orderly manner in the form of written policies, procedures and instructions, and shall include at least the following aspects:", "para_index": 431, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c16cd76f3", "text": "(a)a strategy for regulatory compliance, including compliance with conformity assessment procedures and procedures for the management of modifications to the high-risk AI system;", "para_index": 432, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c8e82a532", "text": "(b)techniques, procedures and systematic actions to be used for the design, design control and design verification of the high-risk AI system;", "para_index": 433, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c39b0fac1", "text": "(c)techniques, procedures and systematic actions to be used for the development, quality control and quality assurance of the high-risk AI system;", "para_index": 434, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c2e980c51", "text": "(d)examination, test and validation procedures to be carried out before, during and after the development of the high-risk AI system, and the frequency with which they have to be carried out;", "para_index": 435, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c6c9b62eb", "text": "(e)technical specifications, including standards, to be applied and, where the relevant harmonised standards are not applied in full, the means to be used to ensure that the high-risk AI system complies with the requirements set out in Chapter 2 of this Title;", "para_index": 436, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c9086d106", "text": "(f)systems and procedures for data management, including data collection, data analysis, data labelling, data storage, data filtration, data mining, data aggregation, data retention and any other operation regarding the data that is performed before and for the purposes of the placing on the market or putting into service of high-risk AI systems;", "para_index": 437, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca87a5ca5", "text": "(g)the risk management system referred to in Article 9;", "para_index": 438, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb503d8f7", "text": "(h)the setting-up, implementation and maintenance of a post-market monitoring system, in accordance with Article 61;", "para_index": 439, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c2e3e6436", "text": "(i)procedures related to the reporting of serious incidents and of malfunctioning in accordance with Article 62;", "para_index": 440, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cfe2d13c8", "text": "(j)the handling of communication with national competent authorities, competent authorities, including sectoral ones, providing or supporting the access to data, notified bodies, other operators, customers or other interested parties;", "para_index": 441, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb672379c", "text": "(k)systems and procedures for record keeping of all relevant documentation and information;", "para_index": 442, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cec67bb85", "text": "(l)resource management, including security of supply related measures;", "para_index": 443, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1a528868", "text": "(m)an accountability framework setting out the responsibilities of the management and other staff with regard to all aspects listed in this paragraph.", "para_index": 444, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c527c48b9", "text": "2.The implementation of aspects referred to in paragraph 1 shall be proportionate to the size of the provider’s organisation.", "para_index": 445, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c22c671dc", "text": "3.For providers that are credit institutions regulated by Directive 2013/36/ EU, the obligation to put a quality management system in place shall be deemed to be fulfilled by complying with the rules on internal governance arrangements, processes and mechanisms pursuant to Article 74 of that Directive. In that context, any harmonised standards referred to in Article 40 of this Regulation shall be taken into account.", "para_index": 446, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c15263d45", "text": "Article 18\nObligation to draw up technical documentation", "para_index": 447, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c22929f5c", "text": "1.Providers of high-risk AI systems shall draw up the technical documentation referred to in Article 11 in accordance with Annex IV.", "para_index": 448, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cef7a0f1d", "text": "2.Providers that are credit institutions regulated by Directive 2013/36/EU shall maintain the technical documentation as part of the documentation concerning internal governance, arrangements, processes and mechanisms pursuant to Article 74 of that Directive.", "para_index": 449, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cbd1d50ea", "text": "Article 19\nConformity assessment", "para_index": 450, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc5f64713", "text": "1.Providers of high-risk AI systems shall ensure that their systems undergo the relevant conformity assessment procedure in accordance with Article 43, prior to their placing on the market or putting into service. Where the compliance of the AI systems with the requirements set out in Chapter 2 of this Title has been demonstrated following that conformity assessment, the providers shall draw up an EU declaration of conformity in accordance with Article 48 and affix the CE marking of conformity i", "para_index": 451, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cfe40f49d", "text": "2.For high-risk AI systems referred to in point 5(b) of Annex III that are placed on the market or put into service by providers that are credit institutions regulated by Directive 2013/36/EU, the conformity assessment shall be carried out as part of the procedure referred to in Articles 97 to101 of that Directive.", "para_index": 452, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce82e44f6", "text": "Article 20\nAutomatically generated logs", "para_index": 453, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce73a1e1f", "text": "1.Providers of high-risk AI systems shall keep the logs automatically generated by their high-risk AI systems, to the extent such logs are under their control by virtue of a contractual arrangement with the user or otherwise by law. The logs shall be kept for a period that is appropriate in the light of the intended purpose of high-risk AI system and applicable legal obligations under Union or national law.", "para_index": 454, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1c5b5d84", "text": "2.Providers that are credit institutions regulated by Directive 2013/36/EU shall maintain the logs automatically generated by their high-risk AI systems as part of the documentation under Articles 74 of that Directive.", "para_index": 455, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cee66b546", "text": "Article 21\nCorrective actions", "para_index": 456, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c00fab709", "text": "Providers of high-risk AI systems which consider or have reason to consider that a high-risk AI system which they have placed on the market or put into service is not in conformity with this Regulation shall immediately take the necessary corrective actions to bring that system into conformity, to withdraw it or to recall it, as appropriate. They shall inform the distributors of the high-risk AI system in question and, where applicable, the authorised representative and importers accordingly.", "para_index": 457, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c4a7cb87a", "text": "Article 22\nDuty of information", "para_index": 458, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c99c39596", "text": "Where the high-risk AI system presents a risk within the meaning of Article 65(1) and that risk is known to the provider of the system, that provider shall immediately inform the national competent authorities of the Member States in which it made the system available and, where applicable, the notified body that issued a certificate for the high-risk AI system, in particular of the non-compliance and of any corrective actions taken.", "para_index": 459, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c8187e4ea", "text": "Article 23\nCooperation with competent authorities", "para_index": 460, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca76a0ec9", "text": "Providers of high-risk AI systems shall, upon request by a national competent authority, provide that authority with all the information and documentation necessary to demonstrate the conformity of the high-risk AI system with the requirements set out in Chapter 2 of this Title, in an official Union language determined by the Member State concerned. Upon a reasoned request from a national competent authority, providers shall also give that authority access to the logs automatically generated by ", "para_index": 461, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cdcdd22b5", "text": "Article 24\nObligations of product manufacturers", "para_index": 462, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c92a7196c", "text": "Where a high-risk AI system related to products to which the legal acts listed in Annex II, section A, apply, is placed on the market or put into service together with the product manufactured in accordance with those legal acts and under the name of the product manufacturer, the manufacturer of the product shall take the responsibility of the compliance of the AI system with this Regulation and, as far as the AI system is concerned, have the same obligations imposed by the present Regulation on", "para_index": 463, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1e518ff3", "text": "Article 25\nAuthorised representatives", "para_index": 464, "entities": [],
