The application of such rules in those Member States shall have an equivalent effect.", "para_index": 786, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c9e8cb3af", "text": "Article 72\nAdministrative fines on Union institutions, agencies and bodies", "para_index": 787, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c9831cf33", "text": "1.The European Data Protection Supervisor may impose administrative fines on Union institutions, agencies and bodies falling within the scope of this Regulation. When deciding whether to impose an administrative fine and deciding on the amount of the administrative fine in each individual case, all relevant circumstances of the specific situation shall be taken into account and due regard shall be given to the following:", "para_index": 788, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c2bae3ba2", "text": "(a)the nature, gravity and duration of the infringement and of its consequences;", "para_index": 789, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf153e57f", "text": "(b)the cooperation with the European Data Protection Supervisor in order to remedy the infringement and mitigate the possible adverse effects of the infringement, including compliance with any of the measures previously ordered by the European Data Protection Supervisor against the Union institution or agency or body concerned with regard to the same subject matter;", "para_index": 790, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ceadf5ed2", "text": "(c)any similar previous infringements by the Union institution, agency or body;", "para_index": 791, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd161865f", "text": "2.The following infringements shall be subject to administrative fines of up to 500 000 EUR:", "para_index": 792, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce9f87333", "text": "(a)non-compliance with the prohibition of the artificial intelligence practices referred to in Article 5;", "para_index": 793, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce4fa4ae8", "text": "(b)non-compliance of the AI system with the requirements laid down in Article 10.", "para_index": 794, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca77b2892", "text": "3.The non-compliance of the AI system with any requirements or obligations under this Regulation, other than those laid down in Articles 5 and 10, shall be subject to administrative fines of up to 250 000 EUR.", "para_index": 795, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc007b70f", "text": "4.Before taking decisions pursuant to this Article, the European Data Protection Supervisor shall give the Union institution, agency or body which is the subject of the proceedings conducted by the European Data Protection Supervisor the opportunity of being heard on the matter regarding the possible infringement. The European Data Protection Supervisor shall base his or her decisions only on elements and circumstances on which the parties concerned have been able to comment. Complainants, if an", "para_index": 796, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c2cd36e9f", "text": "5.The rights of defense of the parties concerned shall be fully respected in the proceedings. They shall be entitled to have access to the European Data Protection Supervisor’s file, subject to the legitimate interest of individuals or undertakings in the protection of their personal data or business secrets.", "para_index": 797, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c17ea2a94", "text": "6.Funds collected by imposition of fines in this Article shall be the income of the general budget of the Union.", "para_index": 798, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc0a738b5", "text": "DELEGATION OF POWER AND COMMITTEE PROCEDURE", "para_index": 799, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cbe9cb545", "text": "Article 73\nExercise of the delegation", "para_index": 800, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c7f3e516d", "text": "1.The power to adopt delegated acts is conferred on the Commission subject to the conditions laid down in this Article.", "para_index": 801, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c8a998d49", "text": "2.The delegation of power referred to in Article 4, Article 7(1), Article 11(3), Article 43(5) and (6) and Article 48(5) shall be conferred on the Commission for an indeterminate period of time from [entering into force of the Regulation].", "para_index": 802, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf13470a3", "text": "3.The delegation of power referred to in Article 4, Article 7(1), Article 11(3), Article 43(5) and (6) and Article 48(5) may be revoked at any time by the European Parliament or by the Council. A decision of revocation shall put an end to the delegation of power specified in that decision. It shall take effect the day following that of its publication in the Official Journal of the European Union or at a later date specified therein. It shall not affect the validity of any delegated acts already", "para_index": 803, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c9d14ce79", "text": "4.As soon as it adopts a delegated act, the Commission shall notify it simultaneously to the European Parliament and to the Council.", "para_index": 804, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c45f96297", "text": "5.Any delegated act adopted pursuant to Article 4, Article 7(1), Article 11(3), Article 43(5) and (6) and Article 48(5) shall enter into force only if no objection has been expressed by either the European Parliament or the Council within a period of three months of notification of that act to the European Parliament and the Council or if, before the expiry of that period, the European Parliament and the Council have both informed the Commission that they will not object. That period shall be ex", "para_index": 805, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c51ddcfc4", "text": "Article 74\nCommittee procedure", "para_index": 806, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd2482dfb", "text": "1.The Commission shall be assisted by a committee. That committee shall be a committee within the meaning of Regulation (EU) No 182/2011.", "para_index": 807, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf1fb232f", "text": "2.Where reference is made to this paragraph, Without prejudice to paragraph 2, when adopting implementing acts pursuant to paragraph 1 concerning Artificial Intelligence systems which are safety components in the meaning of Regulation (EU) YYY/XX [on Artificial Intelligence] of the European Parliament and of the Council*, the requirements set out in Title III, Chapter 2 of that Regulation shall be taken into account.", "para_index": 836, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc370442a", "text": "\\* Regulation (EU) YYY/XX [on Artificial Intelligence] (OJ …).”", "para_index": 837, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c367490f5", "text": "(2) In Article 19, the following paragraph is added:", "para_index": 838, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1369ac0f", "text": "“4. When adopting delegated acts pursuant to paragraphs 1 and 2 concerning Artificial Intelligence systems which are safety components in the meaning of Regulation (EU) YYY/XX [on Artificial Intelligence] , the requirements set out in Title III, Chapter 2 of that Regulation shall be taken into account.”.", "para_index": 847, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf6e24325", "text": "Article 82\nAmendment to Regulation (EU) 2019/2144", "para_index": 848, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce062b122", "text": "In Article 11 of Regulation (EU) 2019/2144, the following paragraph is added:", "para_index": 849, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1fc14d96", "text": "“3. When adopting the implementing acts pursuant to paragraph 2, concerning artificial intelligence systems which are safety components in the meaning of Regulation (EU) YYY/XX [on Artificial Intelligence] of the European Parliament and of the Council*, the requirements set out in Title III, Chapter 2 of that Regulation shall be taken into account.", "para_index": 850, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1cc76222", "text": "\\* Regulation (EU) YYY/XX [on Artificial Intelligence] (OJ …).”.", "para_index": 851, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c0c3c80f7", "text": "Article 83\nAI systems already placed on the market or put into service", "para_index": 852, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c3911c8e4", "text": "1.This Regulation shall not apply to the AI systems which are components of the large-scale IT systems established by the legal acts listed in Annex IX that have been placed on the market or put into service before [12 months after the date of application of this Regulation referred to in Article 85(2)], unless the replacement or amendment of those legal acts leads to a significant change in the design or intended purpose of the AI system or AI systems concerned.", "para_index": 853, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c0d0a6387", "text": "The requirements laid down in this Regulation shall be taken into account, where applicable, in the evaluation of each large-scale IT systems established by the legal acts listed in Annex IX to be undertaken as provided for in those respective acts.", "para_index": 854, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c993bc65a", "text": "2.This Regulation shall apply to the high-risk AI systems, other than the ones referred to in paragraph 1, that have been placed on the market or put into service before [date of application of this Regulation referred to in Article 85(2)], only if, from that date, those systems are subject to significant changes in their design or intended purpose.", "para_index": 855, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c549d54e0", "text": "Article 84\nEvaluation and review", "para_index": 856, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb23f5ca0", "text": "1.The Commission shall assess the need for amendment of the list in Annex III once a year following the entry into force of this Regulation.", "para_index": 857, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1999e92c", "text": "2.By [three years after the date of application of this Regulation referred to in Article 85(2)] and every four years thereafter, the Commission shall submit a report on the evaluation and review of this Regulation to the European Parliament and to the Council. The reports shall be made public.", "para_index": 858, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf707fe19", "text": "3.The reports referred to in paragraph 2 shall devote specific attention to the following:", "para_index": 859, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c0bb1e342", "text": "(a)the status of the financial and human resources of the national competent authorities in order to effectively perform the tasks assigned to them under this Regulation;", "para_index": 860, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c60f955dd", "text": "(b)the state of penalties, and notably administrative fines as referred to in Article 71(1), applied by Member States to infringements of the provisions of this Regulation.", "para_index": 861, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cdd788eb2", "text": "4.Within [three years after the date of application of this Regulation referred to in Article 85(2)] and every four years thereafter, the Commission shall evaluate the impact and effectiveness of codes of conduct to foster the application of the requirements set out in Title III, Chapter 2 and possibly other additional requirements for AI systems other than high-risk AI systems.", "para_index": 862, "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cba46ba98", "text": "1.5.Grounds for the proposal/initiative", "para_index": 946, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c4fea755c", "text": "1.5.1.Requirement(s) to be met in the short or long term including a detailed timeline for roll-out of the implementation of the initiative", "para_index": 947, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c8c5645bd", "text": "The Regulation should be fully applicable one year and a half after its adoption. However, elements of the governance structure should be in place before then. In particular, Member States shall have appointed existing authorities and/or established new authorities performing the tasks set out in the legislation earlier, and the EU AI Board should be set-up and effective. By the time of applicability, the European database of AI systems should be fully operative. In parallel to the adoption proc", "para_index": 948, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c08a0a79a", "text": "1.5.2.Added value of Union involvement (it may result from different factors, e.g. coordination gains, legal certainty, greater effectiveness or complementarities). For the purposes of this point 'added value of Union involvement' is the value resulting from Union intervention which is additional to the value that would have been otherwise created by Member States alone.", "para_index": 949, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca0973400", "text": "An emerging patchy framework of potentially divergent national rules will hamper the seamless provision of AI systems across the EU and is ineffective in ensuring the safety and protection of fundamental rights and Union values across the different Member States. A common EU legislative action on AI could boost the internal market and has great potential to provide European industry with a competitive edge at the global scene and economies of scale that cannot be achieved by individual Member St", "para_index": 950, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cdc9a65e5", "text": "1.5.3.Lessons learned from similar experiences in the past", "para_index": 951, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cfb9acbc0", "text": "The E-commerce Directive 2000/31/EC provides the core framework for the functioning of the single market and the supervision of digital services and sets a basic structure for a general cooperation mechanism among Member States, covering in principle all requirements applicable to digital services. The evaluation of the Directive pointed to shortcomings in several aspects of this cooperation mechanism, including important procedural aspects such as the lack of clear timeframes for response from ", "para_index": 952, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "data", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c3b62a85c", "text": "1.5.4.Compatibility with the Multiannual Financial Framework and possible synergies with other appropriate instruments", "para_index": 953, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cfa70c85b", "text": "The Regulation Laying Down Harmonised Rules on Artificial Intelligence and Amending Certain Union Legislative Acts defines a new common framework of requirements applicable to AI systems, which goes well beyond the framework provided by existing legislation. For this reason, a new national and European regulatory and coordination function needs to be established with this proposal.", "para_index": 954, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cb04a8914", "text": "As regards possible synergies with other appropriate instruments, the role of notifying authorities at national level can be performed by national authorities fulfilling similar functions sunder other EU regulations.", "para_index": 955, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cfa253ad6", "text": "Moreover, by increasing trust in AI and thus encouraging investment in development and adoption of AI, it complements Digital Europe, for which promoting the diffusion of AI is one of five priorities.", "para_index": 956, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c2e29ec5b", "text": "1.5.5.Assessment of the different available financing options, including scope for redeployment", "para_index": 957, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c5e4c3a00", "text": "The staff will be redeployed. The other costs will be supported from the DEP. envelope, given that the objective of this regulation – ensuring trustworthy AI – contributes directly to one key objective of Digital Europe – accelerating AI development and deployment in Europe.", "para_index": 958, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c3026301f", "text": "1.6.Duration and financial impact of the proposal/initiative", "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cda2284a0", "text": "The Regulation will be reviewed and evaluated five years from the entry into force of the regulation. The Commission will report on the findings of the evaluation to the European Parliament, the Council and the European Economic and Social Committee.", "para_index": 982, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c734f43d3", "text": "2.2.Management and control system(s)", "para_index": 983, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c6a9acab1", "text": "2.2.1.Justification of the management mode(s), the funding implementation mechanism(s), the payment modalities and the control strategy proposed", "para_index": 984, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c257bdcd3", "text": "The Regulation establishes a new policy with regard to harmonised rules for the provision of artificial intelligence systems in the internal market while ensuring the respect of safety and fundamental rights. These new rules require a consistency mechanism for the cross-border application of the obligations under this Regulation in the form of a new advisory group coordinating the activities of national authorities.", "para_index": 985, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf84b5474", "text": "In order to face these new tasks, it is necessary to appropriately resource the Commission’s services. The enforcement of the new Regulation is estimated to require 10 FTE à regime (5 FTE for the support to the activities of the Board and 5 FTE for the European Data Protection Supervisor acting as a notifying body for AI systems deployed by a body of the European Union).", "para_index": 986, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf2bf22ab", "text": "2.2.2.Information concerning the risks identified and the internal control system(s) set up to mitigate them", "para_index": 987, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c6dba9f5d", "text": "In order to ensure that the members of the Board have the possibility to make informed analysis on the basis of factual evidence, it is foreseen that the Board should be supported by the administrative structure of the Commission and that an expert group be created to provide additional expertise where required.", "para_index": 988, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1c1173ce", "text": "2.2.3.Estimate and justification of the cost-effectiveness of the controls (ratio of \"control costs ÷ value of the related funds managed\"), and assessment of the expected levels of risk of error (at payment & at closure)", "para_index": 989, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c81b6f214", "text": "For the meeting expenditure, given the low value per transaction (e.g. refunding travel costs for a delegate for a meeting), standard control procedures seem sufficient. Regarding the development of the database, contract attribution has a strong internal control system in place in DG CNECT through centralised procurement activities.", "para_index": 990, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce144ee92", "text": "2.3.Measures to prevent fraud and irregularities", "para_index": 991, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c5ae38948", "text": "Specify existing or envisaged prevention and "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION rationale and assumptions made, also with regard to persons or groups of persons on which the system is intended to be used; the main classification choices; what the system is designed to optimise for and the relevance of the different parameters; the decisions about any possible trade-off made regarding the technical solutions adopted to comply with ", "para_index": 1131, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE including with regard to pre-determined changes as referred to under point (f).", "para_index": 1136, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN description of the procedures in place to ensure that the quality management system remains adequate and effective;", "para_index": 1166, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c29cacc92", "text": "(f)a written declaration that the same application has not been lodged with any other notified body.", "para_index": 1167, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c614ac549", "text": "3.2.The quality management system shall be assessed by the notified body, which shall determine whether it satisfies the requirements referred to in Article 17.", "para_index": 1168, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cefbd6506", "text": "The decision shall be notified to the provider or its authorised representative.", "para_index": 1169, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca37fac8d", "text": "The notification shall contain the conclusions of the assessment of the quality management system and the reasoned assessment decision.", "para_index": 1170, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c1ad8c04e", "text": "3.3.The quality management system as approved shall continue to be implemented and maintained by the provider so that it remains adequate and efficient.", "para_index": 1171, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce1235299", "text": "3.4.Any intended change to the approved quality management system or the list of AI systems covered by the latter shall be brought to the attention of the notified body by the provider.", "para_index": 1172, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c7046b451", "text": "The proposed changes shall be examined by the notified body, which shall decide whether the modified quality management system continues to satisfy the requirements referred to in point 3.2 or whether a reassessment is necessary.", "para_index": 1173, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c642ae871", "text": "The notified body shall notify the provider of its decision. The notification shall contain the conclusions of the examination of the changes and the reasoned assessment decision.", "para_index": 1174, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c90e33a4d", "text": "4.Control of the technical documentation.", "para_index": 1175, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c5403f186", "text": "4.1.In addition to the application referred to in point 3, an application with a notified body of their choice shall be lodged by the provider for the assessment of the technical documentation relating to the AI system which the provider intends to place on the market or put into service and which is covered by the quality management system referred to under point 3.", "para_index": 1176, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cd52678f9", "text": "4.2.The application shall include:", "para_index": 1177, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c53f3e29c", "text": "(a)the name and address of the provider;", "para_index": 1178, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca3a4c9c4", "text": "(b)a written declaration that the same application has not been lodged with any other notified body;", "para_index": 1179, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc5377669", "text": "(c)the technical documentation referred to in Annex IV.", "para_index": 1180, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c9e47df6c", "text": "4.3.The technical documentation shall be examined by the notified body. To this purpose, the notified body shall be granted full access to the training and testing datasets used by the provider, including through application programming interfaces (API) or other appropriate means and tools enabling remote access.", "para_index": 1181, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf64af6ee", "text": "4.4.In examining the technical documentation, the notified body may require that the provider supplies further evidence or carries out further tests so as to enable a proper assessment of conformity of the AI system with the requirements set out in Title III, Chapter 2. Whenever the notified body is not satisfied with the tests carried out by the provider, the notified body shall directly carry out adequate tests, as appropriate.", "para_index": 1182, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ceb1de83d", "text": "4.5.Where necessary to assess the conformity of the high-risk AI system with the requirements set out in Title III, Chapter 2 and upon a reasoned request, the notified body shall also be granted access to the source code of the AI system.", "para_index": 1183, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf954e115", "text": "4.6.The decision shall be notified to the provider or its authorised representative. The notification shall contain the conclusions of the assessment of the technical documentation and the reasoned assessment decision.", "para_index": 1184, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c951ff069", "text": "Where the AI system is in conformity with the requirements set out in Title III, Chapter 2, an EU technical documentation assessment certificate shall be issued by the notified body. The certificate shall indicate the name and address of the provider, the conclusions of the examination, the conditions (if any) for its validity and the data necessary for the identification of the AI system.", "para_index": 1185, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c6e413124", "text": "The certificate and its annexes shall contain all relevant information to allow the conformity of the AI system to be evaluated, and to allow for control of the AI system while in use, where applicable.", "para_index": 1186, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ce6cafa16", "text": "Where the AI system is not in conformity with the requirements set out in Title III, Chapter 2, the notified body shall refuse to issue an EU technical documentation assessment certificate and shall inform the applicant accordingly, giving detailed reasons for its refusal.", "para_index": 1187, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "ca4a5fd23", "text": "Where the AI system does not meet the requirement relating to the data used to train it, re-training of the AI system will be needed prior to the application for a new conformity assessment. In this case, the reasoned assessment decision of the notified body refusing to issue the EU technical documentation assessment certificate shall contain specific considerations on the quality data used to train the AI system, notably on the reasons for non-compliance.", "para_index": 1188, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc1280e20", "text": "4.7.Any change to the AI system that could affect the compliance of the AI system with the requirements or its intended purpose shall be approved by the notified body which issued the EU technical documentation assessment certificate. The provider shall inform such notified body of its intention to introduce any of the above-mentioned changes or if it becomes otherwise aware of the occurrence of such changes. The intended changes shall be assessed by the notified body which shall decide whether ", "para_index": 1189, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c188da4a1", "text": "5.Surveillance of the approved quality management system.", "para_index": 1190, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "c043cb5d9", "text": "5.1.The purpose of the surveillance carried out by the notified body referred to in Point 3 is to make sure that the provider duly fulfils the terms and conditions of the approved quality management system.", "para_index": 1191, "entities": [], "domains": [], "type": "fact", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cbce436e1", "text": "5.2.For assessment purposes, the provider shall allow the notified body to access the premises where the design, development, testing of the AI systems is taking place. The provider shall further share with the notified body all necessary information.", "para_index": 1192, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cc53b0513", "text": "5.3.The notified body shall carry out periodic audits to make sure that the provider maintains and applies the quality management system and shall provide the provider with an audit report. In the context of those audits, the notified body may carry out additional tests of the AI systems for which an EU technical documentation assessment certificate was issued.", "para_index": 1193, "entities": [], "domains": ["ai"], "type": "policy", "source": "/home/agentuser/temp/LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS.md"}, {"id": "cf23ec885", "text": "ANNEX VIII\nINFORMATION TO BE SUBMITTED UPON THE REGISTRATION OF HIGH-RISK AI SYSTEMS IN ACCORDANCE WITH ARTICLE 51", "para_index": 1194, 2021/0106(COD)

Proposal for a

REGULATION OF THE EUROPEAN PARLIAMENT AND OF THE COUNCIL

LAYING DOWN HARMONISED RULES ON ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE ACT) AND AMENDING CERTAIN UNION LEGISLATIVE ACTS

{SEC(2021) 167 final} - {SWD(2021) 84 final} - {SWD(2021) 85 final}

EXPLANATORY MEMORANDUM

1.CONTEXT OF THE PROPOSAL

1.1.Reasons for and objectives of the proposal

This explanatory memorandum accompanies the proposal for a Regulation laying down harmonised rules on artificial intelligence (Artificial Intelligence Act). Artificial Intelligence (AI) is a fast evolving family of technologies that can bring a wide array of economic and societal benefits across the entire spectrum of industries and social activities. By improving prediction, optimising operations and resource allocation, and personalising service delivery, the use of artificial intelligence can support socially and environmentally beneficial outcomes and provide key competitive advantages to companies and the European economy. Such action is especially needed in high-impact sectors, including climate change, environment and health, the public sector, finance, mobility, home affairs and agriculture. However, the same elements and techniques that power the socio-economic benefits of AI can also bring about new risks or negative consequences for individuals or the society. In light of the speed of technological change and possible challenges, the EU is committed to strive for a balanced approach. It is in the Union interest to preserve the EU’s technological leadership and to ensure that Europeans can benefit from new technologies developed and functioning according to Union values, fundamental rights and principles.

This proposal delivers on the political commitment by President von der Leyen, who announced in her political guidelines for the 2019-2024 Commission

[... truncated ...]

 establishing a framework for interoperability between EU information systems in the field of borders and visa (OJ L 135, 22.5.2019, p. 27).
